MoreLessInit = function()
{
var col_height = GetColHeight();
function GetColHeight(){
var col_height = new Array(2);
col_height[0] = 1800;
col_height[1] = $("#floatingarticles_bottom").outerHeight();
return col_height;
}
$('.wgt_floatingarticles').show();
$('.page-left').css
(
{
height: col_height[0],
overflow:'hidden',
display:'block'
}
);
$('.page-marginale').css
(
{
//height: col_height[0]+85,
overflow:'hidden',
display:'block'
}
);
$('.wgt_floatingarticlesmore .showmore').live('click',function()
{
var col_height = GetColHeight();
$(this).fadeOut(300,function(){
$('.wgt_floatingarticlesmore .showless').fadeIn(300);
});
$('.page-left').animate({ height:col_height[1], overflow:'expand'},1000 );
$('.page-marginale').animate({ height:col_height[1]+85, overflow:'expand'},1000 );
});
$('.wgt_floatingarticlesmore .showless').live('click',function()
{
var col_height = GetColHeight();
$(this).fadeOut(300,function(){
$('.wgt_floatingarticlesmore .showmore').fadeIn(300);
});
$('.page-left').animate({ height: col_height[0], overflow:'hidden' },1000 );
$('.page-marginale').animate({ height: col_height[0]+85, overflow:'hidden' },1000 );
});
}
cs_callbacks.push(function(){MoreLessInit();});
</script>
